## Account Addresses

### accountAddressesSchema

`accountAddresses.accountAddressesSchema`

Account Addresses schema (GET /accountAddresses/schema)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.forcemanager.api.accountAddresses.accountAddressesSchema({
	query: { page: 0 },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `never` | No | — |

```ts

}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts

  page?: number,
  where?: string,
  order?: string,
  lang?: string,
  filters?: Record<string, string | number | boolean>
}
```

```ts

  count?: never,
  extraFieldDescription?: never
```

</AccordionGroup>

| `title` | `string` | No | — |
| `type` | `string` | No | — |

### createAccountAddress

`accountAddresses.createAccountAddress`

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.forcemanager.api.accountAddresses.createAccountAddress({
	body: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
}
```

```ts
  order?: never,
  lang?: never,
  filters?: never
}
```

```ts

  count?: never,
  extraFieldDescription?: never

```

### createAccount

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.forcemanager.api.accountsEntity.createAccount({
	body: { branchId: 1, name: 'sample', salesRepId1: 1 },
});
```

```ts
  count?: never,
```

```ts
{
  address1?: string,

  branchId: number,
```
